<script>
var currentOfficeRates = undefined;

var currIdName = {
	"USD": 1,
	"EUR": 2,
	"RUB": 3,
	"GBP": 4,
	"CHF": 5
};

// Название последней выбранной валюты во втором select'e
var lastSelectedSecondCurrencyName = 'EUR';
var canChangeLastSelectedSecondCurrencyName = false;

BX.ready(function() {
	/**
	 * Возвращает курсы обмена в отделении с заданным ID
	 */
	function getOfficeRates(office_id) {
		// Офис
		var office = rates[office_id];
		
		var officeRates = [];
		for(var rate_indx in office.rates) {
			var rate = office.rates[rate_indx];
			
			officeRates.push({
				currency: rate.currency,
				sale: rate.sale,
				purchase: rate.purchase
			});
		}
		// Добавляем валюту "Рубль"
		officeRates.push({
			currency: 'RUB',
			sale: 1,
			purchase: 1
		});
		
		// Заполняем таблицу курсов офиса
		var tableRates = {};

		for(var rate_indx in officeRates) {
			var rate = officeRates[rate_indx];
			
			tableRates[rate.currency] = {};
			
			// Задаем курс каждой валюты относительно всех других
			for(var rate_indx in officeRates) {
				var rate2 = officeRates[rate_indx];
				
				// Первый индекс - валюта продажи, второй - покупки
				if (rate.currency == rate2.currency) {
					tableRates[rate.currency][rate2.currency] = 1;
				} else {
					tableRates[rate.currency][rate2.currency] = rate.sale / rate2.purchase;
				}
			}
		}
		
		return tableRates;
	}

	var selct = $('#officeAdresses');
	//console.log(' --- ');
	//console.log(rates);
	//console.log(JSON.stringify(rates));

	// Задаем option'ы select'a
	for(var index in rates) {
		//console.log('index: ' + index);
		//console.log(JSON.stringify(rates[index]));
		var city = rates[index];
		var option = $('<option value="'+city['id']+'" >' + city['name'] + '</option>');
		selct.append(option);
	}

	
	selct.bind('change', {'data': '123'}, function(e) {
		$('#office-cur-rates').html('');

		var isUp = false;
		var cls = '';
		var city = rates[$(this).val()];
		var sbl = '';
		
		// /////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
		// Задаем курсы обмена валют выбранного офиса
		currentOfficeRates = getOfficeRates($(this).val());
		//console.log(currentOfficeRates);
		var selected_currency_name = $(".currency-type-2 option:selected").text();

		// Обновляем option'ы select'ов
		$(".currency-type-2").find('option').remove();
		$(".currency-type-3").find('option').remove();

		for(var rate_indx in currentOfficeRates) {
			$(".currency-type-2").append('<option value="' + currIdName[rate_indx] + '">' + rate_indx + '</option>');

			if (selected_currency_name != rate_indx)
				$(".currency-type-3").append('<option value="' + currIdName[rate_indx] + '">' + rate_indx + '</option>');
		}
		
		$("#input_field").val("");
		$("#input_field1").val("");

		//$(".currency-type-2").change();
		//$(".currency-type-3").change();
		/*
		$('select').niceSelect('update');

		$("div.currency-type-2").children("ul.list").children("li:first").click();
		$("div.currency-type-3").children("ul.list").children("li:first").click();
		$("body").click();
*/
		////$('select').niceSelect('update');
		$(".currency-type-2").niceSelect('update');
		$(".currency-type-3").niceSelect('update');

		//$(".currency-type-2").change();
		//$(".currency-type-3").change();

		//$('select').niceSelect('update');
		//$('select').change();
		// -----------------------------		
		// \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\
		
		
		for(var index in city.rates) {
			var rate = city.rates[index];
			var tr = $('<tr>');
			var td1 = $('<td class="currency_name">' + rate['currency'] + '</td>');

			//console.log(JSON.stringify(rate));
			rate['purchase'] = Number(rate['purchase']).toFixed(2);
			isUp = Number(rate['purchase']) > Number(rate['p_purchase']);
			cls = isUp?'currency_up':'currency_down';
			sbl = isUp?'ˆ':'ˇ';
			var td2 = $('<td class="currency_buy">' + String(rate['purchase']).replace(".", ",") + '<span class="'+cls+'">'+sbl+'</span></td>');

			rate['sale'] = Number(rate['sale']).toFixed(2);
			isUp = Number(rate['sale']) > Number(rate['p_sale']);
			cls = isUp?'currency_up':'currency_down';
			sbl = isUp?'ˆ':'ˇ';
			var td3 = $('<td class="currency_sell">' + String(rate['sale']).replace(".", ",") + '<span class="'+cls+'">'+sbl+'</span></td>');

			tr.append(td1);
			tr.append(td2);
			tr.append(td3);

			$('#office-cur-rates').append(tr);
		}
	});

	
	$(".currency-type-2").change(function() {
		canChangeLastSelectedSecondCurrencyName = false;
		var selected_currency_name = $(".currency-type-2 option:selected").text();

		$(".currency-type-3").find('option').remove();

		for(var rate_indx in currentOfficeRates) {
			if (selected_currency_name != rate_indx)
				$(".currency-type-3").append(
					'<option value="' + currIdName[rate_indx] + '"' + 
					//(lastSelectedSecondCurrencyName == rate_indx ? " selected" : "") + 
					'>' + rate_indx + '</option>');
		}


		$(".currency-type-3 option[value='" + currIdName[lastSelectedSecondCurrencyName] + "']").attr('selected', 'true');

		$("#input_field1").val("");

		$(".currency-type-2").niceSelect('update');
		$(".currency-type-3").niceSelect('update');		

		$("div.currency-type-3").children("ul.list").children("li.selected").click();
		$("body").click();

		$(".currency-type-3").niceSelect('update');

		canChangeLastSelectedSecondCurrencyName = true;
	});

	
	$(".currency-type-3").change(function() {
		if (canChangeLastSelectedSecondCurrencyName)
			lastSelectedSecondCurrencyName = $(".currency-type-3 option:selected").text();
	});

	
	selct.val(5);
	$(selct).niceSelect();
	$(selct).change();

	$("#input_field").val("");
	$("#input_field1").val("");

	$('select').niceSelect('update');

	$("div .currency-type-2").change();
	$("div .currency-type-3").change();

});
</script>
